### ANNEX I, first paragraph

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| REQUIREMENTS TO BE MET BY CONFORMITY ASSESSMENT BODIES | REQUIREMENTS TO BE MET BY CONFORMITY ASSESSMENT BODIES |

### ANNEX I, point 1.

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| 1. A conformity assessment body shall be established under national law and shall have legal personality. | 1. A conformity assessment body shall be established under national law and shall have legal personality. |

### ANNEX I, point 2.

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| 2. A conformity assessment body shall not be a high-risk supplier. | 2. A conformity assessment body shall not be a high-risk supplier. |

### ANNEX I, point 3.

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| 3. A conformity assessment body shall be a third-party body that is independent of the entity or the ICT products, ICT services, ICT processes or managed security services that it assesses. A body belonging to a business association or professional federation representing undertakings involved in the design, manufacturing, provision, assembly, use or maintenance of ICT products, ICT services, ICT processes or managed security services that it assesses, may, on condition that its independence and the absence of any conflict of interest are demonstrated, be considered to be such a third-party body. | 3. A conformity assessment body shall be a third-party body that is independent of the entity or the ICT products, ICT services, ICT processes or managed security services that it assesses. A body belonging to a business association or professional federation representing undertakings involved in the design, manufacturing, provision, assembly, use or maintenance of ICT products, ICT services, ICT processes or managed security services that it assesses, may, on condition that its independence and the absence of any conflict of interest are demonstrated, be considered to be such a third-party body. |

### ANNEX I, point 4.

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| 4. A conformity assessment body, its top-level management and the personnel responsible for carrying out conformity assessment tasks shall not be the designer, manufacturer, supplier, installer, purchaser, owner, user or maintainer of the ICT products, ICT services, ICT processes, managed security services or entities that they assess, nor the authorised representative of any of those parties. This shall not preclude the use of the ICT products, ICT services, ICT processes or managed security services assessed that are necessary for the operations of the conformity assessment body or the use of such ICT products, ICT services, ICT processes or managed security services for personal purposes. | 4. A conformity assessment body, its top-level management and the personnel responsible for carrying out conformity assessment tasks shall not be the designer, manufacturer, supplier, installer, purchaser, owner, user or maintainer of the ICT products, ICT services, ICT processes, managed security services or entities that they assess, nor the authorised representative of any of those parties. This shall not preclude the use of the ICT products, ICT services, ICT processes or managed security services assessed that are necessary for the operations of the conformity assessment body or the use of such ICT products, ICT services, ICT processes or managed security services for personal purposes. |

### ANNEX I, point 5.

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| 5. A conformity assessment body, its top-level management and the personnel responsible for carrying out conformity assessment tasks shall not be directly involved in the design, manufacture or construction, the marketing, installation, use or maintenance of the ICT products, ICT services, ICT processes, managed security services or entities they are assess, or represent parties engaged in those activities. They shall not engage in any activity that may conflict with their independence of judgement or integrity in relation to their conformity assessment activities. This shall in particular apply to consultancy services. | 5. A conformity assessment body, its top-level management and the personnel responsible for carrying out conformity assessment tasks shall not be directly involved in the design, manufacture or construction, the marketing, installation, use or maintenance of the ICT products, ICT services, ICT processes, managed security services or entities they are assess, or represent parties engaged in those activities. They shall not engage in any activity that may conflict with their independence of judgement or integrity in relation to their conformity assessment activities. This shall in particular apply to consultancy services. |

### ANNEX I, point 6.

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| 6. Conformity assessment bodies shall ensure that the activities of their subsidiaries and subcontractors do not affect the confidentiality, objectivity or impartiality of their conformity assessment activities. | 6. Conformity assessment bodies shall ensure that the activities of their subsidiaries and subcontractors do not affect the confidentiality, objectivity or impartiality of their conformity assessment activities. |

### ANNEX I, point 7.

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| 7. If a conformity assessment body is owned or operated by a public entity or institution, independence and absence of any conflict of interest shall be ensured between the national cybersecurity certification authority and the conformity assessment body, and shall be documented. | 7. If a conformity assessment body is owned or operated by a public entity or institution, independence and absence of any conflict of interest shall be ensured between the national cybersecurity certification authority and the conformity assessment body, and shall be documented. |

### ANNEX I, point 8.

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| 8. Conformity assessment bodies and their personnel shall carry out conformity assessment activities with the highest degree of professional integrity and the requisite technical competence in the specific field, and shall be free from all pressures and inducements, particularly of a financial nature, that might influence their judgement or the results of their conformity assessment activities, especially as regards persons or groups of persons with an interest in the results of those activities. | 8. Conformity assessment bodies and their personnel shall carry out conformity assessment activities with the highest degree of professional integrity and the requisite technical competence in the specific field, and shall be free from all pressures and inducements, particularly of a financial nature, that might influence their judgement or the results of their conformity assessment activities, especially as regards persons or groups of persons with an interest in the results of those activities. |

### ANNEX I, point 9.

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| 9. A conformity assessment body shall be capable of carrying out all the conformity assessment tasks assigned to it under this Regulation, regardless of whether those tasks are carried out by the conformity assessment body itself or on its behalf and under its responsibility. Any subcontracting to, or consultation of, external staff shall be properly documented, shall not involve any intermediaries and shall be subject to a written agreement covering, among other things, confidentiality and conflicts of interest. | 9. A conformity assessment body shall be capable of carrying out all the conformity assessment tasks assigned to it under this Regulation, regardless of whether those tasks are carried out by the conformity assessment body itself or on its behalf and under its responsibility. Any subcontracting to, or consultation of, external staff shall be properly documented, shall not involve any intermediaries and shall be subject to a written agreement covering, among other things, confidentiality and conflicts of interest. |

### ANNEX I, point 10.

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| 10. At all times and for each conformity assessment procedure and each type, category or sub-category of ICT products, ICT services, ICT processes, managed security services or entities, a conformity assessment body shall have at its disposal the necessary: | 10. At all times and for each conformity assessment procedure and each type, category or sub-category of ICT products, ICT services, ICT processes, managed security services or entities, a conformity assessment body shall have at its disposal the necessary: |

### ANNEX I, point 10.(a)

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| (a) personnel with technical knowledge and sufficient and appropriate experience to perform the conformity assessment tasks; | (a) personnel with technical knowledge and sufficient and appropriate experience to perform the conformity assessment tasks; |

### ANNEX I, point 10.(b)

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| (b) descriptions of procedures in accordance with which conformity assessment is to be carried out, ensuring the transparency of and ability to reproduce those procedures; the conformity assessment body shall have appropriate policies and procedures in place that distinguish between tasks that it carries out as a body notified pursuant to Article 93 and its other activities; | (b) descriptions of procedures in accordance with which conformity assessment is to be carried out, ensuring the transparency of and ability to reproduce those procedures; the conformity assessment body shall have appropriate policies and procedures in place that distinguish between tasks that it carries out as a body notified pursuant to Article 93 and its other activities; |

### ANNEX I, point 10.(c)

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| (c) procedures for the performance of activities that take due account of the size of an undertaking, the sector in which it operates, its structure, the degree of complexity of the technology of the ICT product, ICT service or ICT process in question and the mass or serial nature of the production process. | (c) procedures for the performance of activities that take due account of the size of an undertaking, the sector in which it operates, its structure, the degree of complexity of the technology of the ICT product, ICT service or ICT process in question and the mass or serial nature of the production process. |

### ANNEX I, point 11.

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| 11. A conformity assessment body shall have the means necessary to perform the technical and administrative tasks connected with the conformity assessment activities in an appropriate manner, and shall have access to all necessary equipment and facilities. | 11. A conformity assessment body shall have the means necessary to perform the technical and administrative tasks connected with the conformity assessment activities in an appropriate manner, and shall have access to all necessary equipment and facilities. |

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| 12. A conformity assessment body shall not use, install or otherwise integrate ICT components or components that include ICT components in key ICT assets identified pursuant to Article 102 from high-risk suppliers, for conformity assessment activities under Title III. | 12. | Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| 13. The personnel responsible for carrying out conformity assessment activities shall have the following: | 13. The personnel responsible for carrying out conformity assessment activities shall have the following: |

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |

| --- | --- |
| (c) appropriate knowledge and understanding of the applicable requirements and standards, and of the relevant provisions of Union harmonisation legislation and of its implementing acts; | (c) appropriate knowledge and understanding of the applicable requirements and standards, and of the relevant provisions of Union harmonisation legislation and of its implementing acts; |

| --- | --- |
| (d) the ability to draw up certificates, records and reports demonstrating that conformity assessments have been carried out. | (d) the ability to draw up certificates, records and reports demonstrating that conformity assessments have been carried out. |

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| 14. Conformity assessment bodies, their top-level management, the personnel responsible for carrying out conformity assessment activities, and any subcontractors shall be impartial. | 14. Conformity assessment bodies, their top-level management, the personnel responsible for carrying out conformity assessment activities, and any subcontractors shall be impartial. |

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |
| 17. The personnel of a conformity assessment body shall observe professional secrecy with regard to all information obtained in carrying out their tasks under this Regulation or any provision of Member State law giving effect to it, except where disclosure is required by Union or Member State law to which such persons are subject, and except in relation to the competent authorities of the Member States in which they carry out their activities. The personnel of a conformity assessment body shall observe professional secrecy with regard to all information obtained in carrying out their tasks under this Regulation or any provision of Member State law giving effect to it, except where disclosure is required by Union or Member State law to which such persons are subject, and except in relation to the competent authorities of the Member States in which they carry out their activities. | Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |

Conformity assessment bodies shall operate in accordance with a set of consistent, fair, proportionate and reasonable terms and conditions, while avoiding unnecessary burden for economic operators, taking into account the interests of microenterprises and small to medium-sized enterprises in relation to fees. | 18. | --- | --- |

| Commission Proposal | Council Mandate |
| --- | --- |

| --- | --- |
